- A Tumar is an amulet used by the Kazakh people  
  _[kk]_ ← "Тұмар - зиянды тылсым күштерден, тіл-көзден сақтайды деп сенген ежелден келе жатқан қазақ халқының бұйымы."
- The Kazakh people have used Tumars since ancient times  
  _[kk]_ ← "Тұмар - зиянды тылсым күштерден, тіл-көзден сақтайды деп сенген ежелден келе жатқан қазақ халқының бұйымы."
- Tumars are believed to protect against evil invisible forces

- Ancient Turkic people carried soil from their birthplace in the tumar

- This decline is due to the influence of Islam  
  _[kk]_ ← "Ислам дінінің әсерімен, тұмарға туған жердің топырағын салу ығыстырылап, орнына Құран аяттары жазылған қағаз салу белең алған."

  _[lt]_ ← "Aptinkami daugiausia laidojimo paminkluose."
- Amulets were most often made of amber  
  _[lt]_ ← "Dažniausiai gaminti iš gintaro (Palangos kapinynai, Šventosios gyvenvietės) ir žvėrių dantų su pragręžta skylute, skirta pakabinti arba prisiūti prie drabužių."
- Amulets were found in the Palanga cemetery  
  _[lt]_ ← "Dažniausiai gaminti iš gintaro (Palangos kapinynai, Šventosios gyvenvietės) ir žvėrių dantų su pragręžta skylute, skirta pakabinti arba prisiūti prie drabužių."
- Amulets were found in the Svetosios settlement  
  _[lt]_ ← "Dažniausiai gaminti iš gintaro (Palangos kapinynai, Šventosios gyvenvietės) ir žvėrių dantų su pragręžta skylute, skirta pakabinti arba prisiūti prie drabužių."
- Amulets were also made from animal teeth  
  _[lt]_ ← "Dažniausiai gaminti iš gintaro (Palangos kapinynai, Šventosios gyvenvietės) ir žvėrių dantų su pragręžta skylute, skirta pakabinti arba prisiūti prie drabužių."
- The animal teeth had a drilled hole  
  _[lt]_ ← "Dažniausiai gaminti iš gintaro (Palangos kapinynai, Šventosios gyvenvietės) ir žvėrių dantų su pragręžta skylute, skirta pakabinti arba prisiūti prie drabužių."
- Comb-shaped pendants are particularly distinctive  
  _[lt]_ ← "Daryti įvairių formų; ypač išsiskiria vyrų (karių) kapuose randami šukučių pavidalo kabučiai."
- Comb-shaped pendants were found in men's graves  
  _[lt]_ ← "Daryti įvairių formų; ypač išsiskiria vyrų (karių) kapuose randami šukučių pavidalo kabučiai."
- Men's graves referred to as warriors' graves  
  _[lt]_ ← "Daryti įvairių formų; ypač išsiskiria vyrų (karių) kapuose randami šukučių pavidalo kabučiai."
- Circular plates were found at the head of women's graves  
  _[lt]_ ← "Moterų kapų galvūgalyje randama apskritų plokštelių, kurios greičiausiai buvo įsukamos į plaukų kuodą ir turėjo saugoti nuo galvos skausmų ar kitų negalavimų."
- These plates were likely inserted into hair buns  
  _[lt]_ ← "Moterų kapų galvūgalyje randama apskritų plokštelių, kurios greičiausiai buvo įsukamos į plaukų kuodą ir turėjo saugoti nuo galvos skausmų ar kitų negalavimų."
- The plates were intended to protect against headaches  
  _[lt]_ ← "Moterų kapų galvūgalyje randama apskritų plokštelių, kurios greičiausiai buvo įsukamos į plaukų kuodą ir turėjo saugoti nuo galvos skausmų ar kitų negalavimų."
- Amulets were considered a symbol of luck for hunters  
  _[lt]_ ← "Amuletai laikyti ir medžiotojų sėkmės ženklu."
- Amulets are placed in vehicles  
  _[lv]_ ← "Amuletus nēsā uz ķermeņa (bieži vien kā rotaslietas), uz apģērba (kā aksesuārus), novieto transportlīdzekļos vai dzīvesvietā."
- Amulets are often made from hunted materials such as teeth and bones  
  _[lv]_ ← "Amuletus cilvēki izmanto jau kopš pirmatnējo kopienu laikiem, bieži vien izgatavojot no medījuma (zobi un kauli), tika uzskatīts, ka tie var nēsātājam piešķirt …"
- Amulets are most commonly found in the form of necklaces  
  _[lv]_ ← "Visbiežāk amuletus var sastapt kaklarotu veidolā."
- Amulets are most commonly precious stones or ordinary stones  
  _[mk]_ ← "Најчесто амајлиите се: благороден камен или обичен камен, статуа, паричка, цртеж, висок, прстен, билка, животно, итн."
- Even a word spoken in a specific case can reject evil or bad luck  
  _[mk]_ ← "дека дури и збор кажан во конкретен случај, како на пример: ваде ретро сатана — (латински, "Оди си сатано"), го отфрла злото или лошата среќа."
- This choice was based on a belief regarding the crystals' reaction to poison  
  _[nl]_ ← "De keuze van bergkristal als drinkglas of als deel van middeleeuwse sieraden kan worden toegeschreven aan het geloof dat deze kristallen zouden breken of verkle…"
- The belief held that these crystals would break when exposed to poison  
  _[nl]_ ← "De keuze van bergkristal als drinkglas of als deel van middeleeuwse sieraden kan worden toegeschreven aan het geloof dat deze kristallen zouden breken of verkle…"
- The belief held that these crystals would change color when exposed to poison  
  _[nl]_ ← "De keuze van bergkristal als drinkglas of als deel van middeleeuwse sieraden kan worden toegeschreven aan het geloof dat deze kristallen zouden breken of verkle…"
- The object is a Totem  
  _[nl]_ ← "Fylacterion Mascotte Apotropaeon Bijgeloof Totem Talisman van Karel de Grote"
- The object is a Talisman  
  _[nl]_ ← "Fylacterion Mascotte Apotropaeon Bijgeloof Totem Talisman van Karel de Grote"
- The object is associated with Charlemagne  
  _[nl]_ ← "Fylacterion Mascotte Apotropaeon Bijgeloof Totem Talisman van Karel de Grote"
- The item is called 'De draak met de zeven koppen'  
  _[nl]_ ← "De draak met de zeven koppen"
- Amulets play an important role in primitive religions  
  _[pl]_ ← "Odgrywa istotną rolę w religiach pierwotnych (zbliżony do idoli)."
- Similar solar deity amulets were discovered in Europe  
  _[ro]_ ← "Alte amulete reprezentau zeități solare, ca cele descoperite în Europa, din epoca bronzului și epoca fierului."
- These European amulets date from the Bronze Age and the Iron Age  
  _[ro]_ ← "Alte amulete reprezentau zeități solare, ca cele descoperite în Europa, din epoca bronzului și epoca fierului."
- An amulet was found in the Brînzeni cave  
  _[ro]_ ← "Pe teritoriul Republicii Moldova, în grota de la Brînzeni a fost găsită o amuletă, care a fost datată cu cca."
- The Brînzeni cave is located within the territory of the Republic of Moldova  
  _[ro]_ ← "Pe teritoriul Republicii Moldova, în grota de la Brînzeni a fost găsită o amuletă, care a fost datată cu cca."
- Amulets are placed in vehicles  
  _[ru]_ ← "Амулеты носят на теле (часто как украшение) или на одежде, помещают в транспортные средства или в жилище, либо вешают вокруг скота."
- Amulets are hung around livestock  
  _[ru]_ ← "Амулеты носят на теле (часто как украшение) или на одежде, помещают в транспортные средства или в жилище, либо вешают вокруг скота."
- Amulets are used as love charms  
  _[ru]_ ← "Амулеты находят применение в нетрадиционной медицине, в качестве защиты беременных, против «сглаза» и в качестве любовных чар."
- Amulets are used in non-traditional medicine  
  _[ru]_ ← "Амулеты находят применение в нетрадиционной медицине, в качестве защиты беременных, против «сглаза» и в качестве любовных чар."
- German mythology uses Hercules' club as an amulet  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- German mythology uses Mjolnir as an amulet  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- Native Americans of North America used a medicine bag as an amulet  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- Americans use a rabbit's foot as an amulet  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- Native Americans use dream catchers as amulets  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- A talisman is a religious fetish  
  _[ru]_ ← "Талисман Религиозный фетишизм Монета-амулет Науз — оберег у славян"
- The Nauz coin is an amulet  
  _[ru]_ ← "Талисман Религиозный фетишизм Монета-амулет Науз — оберег у славян"
- The Nauz coin was used as a protective charm by Slavs  
  _[ru]_ ← "Талисман Религиозный фетишизм Монета-амулет Науз — оберег у славян"
- Ancient moon amulets are a classification of Slavic protective charms  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древние амулеты-лунницы — классификация и типология оберегов Древнерусские привески и амулеты XI—XIII веков"
- Ancient Russian pendants and amulets from the 11th to 13th centuries exist  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древние амулеты-лунницы — классификация и типология оберегов Древнерусские привески и амулеты XI—XIII веков"
- Cursing can ward off evil or bad luck  
  _[sl]_ ← "Potencialni amuleti so dragulji, zlasti vgravirani dragulji, kipi, kovanci, risbe, obeski, prstani, rastline in živali; celo besedilo v obliki čarobnega uroka a…"
- Another example of such an inscription is 'sreča uporabniku'  
  _[sl]_ ← "Notranja moč amuleta je razvidna tudi iz napisov, kot je vterfexix (utere fexix) ali "sreča uporabniku"."
- The Thetford treasure was discovered in Norfolk, Great Britain  
  _[sl]_ ← "Kot amuletni predmeti so se lahko uporabljali tudi taki, kot so jih našli v Thetford treasure, Norfolk, Velika Britanija, in so bili zlati predmeti namenjeni ko…"
- An amulet can also be a captured trophy  
  _[sv]_ ← "En amulett kan också vara en erövrad trofé, exempelvis klor och tänder från vilda djur, smycken, stenar eller mineraler."
- Examples of such trophies include claws and teeth from wild animals  
  _[sv]_ ← "En amulett kan också vara en erövrad trofé, exempelvis klor och tänder från vilda djur, smycken, stenar eller mineraler."
- Examples of such trophies include jewelry  
  _[sv]_ ← "En amulett kan också vara en erövrad trofé, exempelvis klor och tänder från vilda djur, smycken, stenar eller mineraler."
- Examples of such trophies include stones or minerals  
  _[sv]_ ← "En amulett kan också vara en erövrad trofé, exempelvis klor och tänder från vilda djur, smycken, stenar eller mineraler."
- Apotropaic objects are items used in folk beliefs  
  _[zh]_ ← "厭勝物是民間信仰中,作為趨吉避凶的物品,並常帶有一定之宗教色彩。"
- These items serve to attract good fortune and ward off evil  
  _[zh]_ ← "厭勝物是民間信仰中,作為趨吉避凶的物品,並常帶有一定之宗教色彩。"

### Islam
- Arabs named these items 'amulets' because they believed it was the completion of medicine and healing  
  _[ar]_ ← "التميمة وهي خرزات أو عظام أو كتابات أو خيوط أو غيرها تعلق في أعناق الصبيان وغيرهم أو في العضد أو على البيوت أو السيارات بغرض دفع البلاء, أو رفع ذلك بعد وقوعه، و…"
- The Prophet Muhammad stated that amulets are shirk  
  _[ar]_ ← "وعن ابن مسعود رضي الله عنه قال: سمعت رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم يقول: (إنَّ الرُّقى ، و التَّمائمَ ، و التِّوَلَةَ شركٌ)."
- The Prophet Muhammad stated that charms are shirk  
  _[ar]_ ← "وعن ابن مسعود رضي الله عنه قال: سمعت رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم يقول: (إنَّ الرُّقى ، و التَّمائمَ ، و التِّوَلَةَ شركٌ)."
- The Prophet Muhammad stated that protective amulets are shirk  
  _[ar]_ ← "وعن ابن مسعود رضي الله عنه قال: سمعت رسول الله صلى الله عليه وسلم يقول: (إنَّ الرُّقى ، و التَّمائمَ ، و التِّوَلَةَ شركٌ)."
- Abu Dawud narrated this hadith  
  _[ar]_ ← "رواه أبو داود وصححه الألباني"
- Al-Albani authenticated this hadith  
  _[ar]_ ← "رواه أبو داود وصححه الألباني"
- The use of amulets was common and widespread among Arabs in the pre-Islamic period (Jahiliyyah)  
  _[ar]_ ← "كان استعمال التمائم بأنواعها شائعاً منتشراً عند العرب في جاهليتم, وذكرها منتشر في أشعارهم وأخبارهم, ويزعمون أنها تحفظهم, وتدفع عنهم أذى العين والجن والحاسدين قب…"
- Amulets are mentioned in their poetry and historical accounts  
  _[ar]_ ← "كان استعمال التمائم بأنواعها شائعاً منتشراً عند العرب في جاهليتم, وذكرها منتشر في أشعارهم وأخبارهم, ويزعمون أنها تحفظهم, وتدفع عنهم أذى العين والجن والحاسدين قب…"
- People believed that amulets would remove misfortune after it had befallen them  
  _[ar]_ ← "كان استعمال التمائم بأنواعها شائعاً منتشراً عند العرب في جاهليتم, وذكرها منتشر في أشعارهم وأخبارهم, ويزعمون أنها تحفظهم, وتدفع عنهم أذى العين والجن والحاسدين قب…"
- When the children grew up, they removed the amulets from them  
  _[ar]_ ← "كان استعمال التمائم بأنواعها شائعاً منتشراً عند العرب في جاهليتم, وذكرها منتشر في أشعارهم وأخبارهم, ويزعمون أنها تحفظهم, وتدفع عنهم أذى العين والجن والحاسدين قب…"
- One type involved hanging a rabbit's heel on oneself  
  _[ar]_ ← "كان استعمال التمائم بأنواعها شائعاً منتشراً عند العرب في جاهليتم, وذكرها منتشر في أشعارهم وأخبارهم, ويزعمون أنها تحفظهم, وتدفع عنهم أذى العين والجن والحاسدين قب…"
- Another type involved hanging a fox or cat's tooth on young boys  
  _[ar]_ ← "كان استعمال التمائم بأنواعها شائعاً منتشراً عند العرب في جاهليتم, وذكرها منتشر في أشعارهم وأخبارهم, ويزعمون أنها تحفظهم, وتدفع عنهم أذى العين والجن والحاسدين قب…"
- Another type involved hanging strings on animals such as horses and camels  
  _[ar]_ ← "كان استعمال التمائم بأنواعها شائعاً منتشراً عند العرب في جاهليتم, وذكرها منتشر في أشعارهم وأخبارهم, ويزعمون أنها تحفظهم, وتدفع عنهم أذى العين والجن والحاسدين قب…"
- For animal protection, they used an old bowstring that had been broken  
  _[ar]_ ← "كان استعمال التمائم بأنواعها شائعاً منتشراً عند العرب في جاهليتم, وذكرها منتشر في أشعارهم وأخبارهم, ويزعمون أنها تحفظهم, وتدفع عنهم أذى العين والجن والحاسدين قب…"
- The purpose of hanging the bowstring on animals was to ward off the evil eye and calamities from the animal  
  _[ar]_ ← "كان استعمال التمائم بأنواعها شائعاً منتشراً عند العرب في جاهليتم, وذكرها منتشر في أشعارهم وأخبارهم, ويزعمون أنها تحفظهم, وتدفع عنهم أذى العين والجن والحاسدين قب…"
- The text refers to 'Raqiya Shar'iyya'  
  _[ar]_ ← "رقية شرعية"
- Islam emphasizes that help should be sought only from Allah  
  _[az]_ ← "İslam inancı isə köməyin yalnız Allahdan dilənməsi vurğulamışdır və tilsimlere çox da çox icazə verilməmişdir."
- Amulets among Arabs are leather pouches  
  _[de]_ ← "Bei den Arabern sind Amulette Ledertäschchen mit eingenähtem Papier, auf das eine Koransure oder ein magisches Zeichen geschrieben ist."
- The paper is sewn into the leather pouches  
  _[de]_ ← "Bei den Arabern sind Amulette Ledertäschchen mit eingenähtem Papier, auf das eine Koransure oder ein magisches Zeichen geschrieben ist."
- A verse from the Quran or a magical symbol is written on the paper  
  _[de]_ ← "Bei den Arabern sind Amulette Ledertäschchen mit eingenähtem Papier, auf das eine Koransure oder ein magisches Zeichen geschrieben ist."
- Amulets spread the Islamic blessing of Baraka  
  _[de]_ ← "Sie verbreiten die islamische Segenskraft Baraka."
- Qusta ibn Luqa wrote a treatise on the value of amulets around 900 AD  
  _[de]_ ← "Der Bagdader Mathematiker, Philosoph und Arzt Qusta ibn Luqa (Qusṭā ibn Lūqā al-Baʿlabakkī) war melchitischer Christ griechischer Abstammung und machte bereits …"
- In his treatise, Qusta ibn Luqa attributed the effect of amulets to belief and human imagination  
  _[de]_ ← "Der Bagdader Mathematiker, Philosoph und Arzt Qusta ibn Luqa (Qusṭā ibn Lūqā al-Baʿlabakkī) war melchitischer Christ griechischer Abstammung und machte bereits …"
- He denied occult properties  
  _[de]_ ← "Okkulte oder astrale Eigenschaften verneinte er."
- He denied astral properties  
  _[de]_ ← "Okkulte oder astrale Eigenschaften verneinte er."
- Rudolf Kriß wrote a work titled 'Volksglaube im Bereich des Islam'  
  _[de]_ ← "Rudolf Kriß: Volksglaube im Bereich des Islam."
- The work focuses on popular belief within the context of Islam  
  _[de]_ ← "Rudolf Kriß: Volksglaube im Bereich des Islam."
- The work is titled 'Amulets, Magic Formulas, and Incantations'  
  _[de]_ ← "Band 2: Amulette, Zauberformeln und Beschwörungen."
- This work is Volume 2 of a series  
  _[de]_ ← "Band 2: Amulette, Zauberformeln und Beschwörungen."
- The work was published by Harrassowitz  
  _[de]_ ← "Harrassowitz, Wiesbaden 1962, ."
- The publication location is Wiesbaden  
  _[de]_ ← "Harrassowitz, Wiesbaden 1962, ."
- The work was published in 1962  
  _[de]_ ← "Harrassowitz, Wiesbaden 1962, ."
- The use of amulets is considered superstitious  
  _[es]_ ← "Sin embargo, el uso de amuletos y de otros talismanes se considera supersticioso entre los musulmanes más radicales."
- This view is held by the most radical Muslims  
  _[es]_ ← "Sin embargo, el uso de amuletos y de otros talismanes se considera supersticioso entre los musulmanes más radicales."
- The 'Imam Javad' amulet is an example of such a case  
  _[fa]_ ← "در بعضی موارد همانند «حرز امام جواد» تعویذ به بازوی راست، بسته می‌شود."
- A 'balagardan' is a prayer  
  _[fa]_ ← "بلاگردان وسیله یا دعایی که باعث دوری شر از کسی شود."
- The purpose of a balagardan is to ward off evil from a person  
  _[fa]_ ← "بلاگردان وسیله یا دعایی که باعث دوری شر از کسی شود."
- In Turkey, the Nazar (evil eye) amulet is widely used  
  _[fa]_ ← "مثلاً در ترکیه نظرقربانی به‌طور وسیعی برای جلوگیری از چشم زخم استفاده می‌شود."
- The Nazar amulet is used to prevent the evil eye  
  _[fa]_ ← "مثلاً در ترکیه نظرقربانی به‌طور وسیعی برای جلوگیری از چشم زخم استفاده می‌شود."
- Many ethnic groups have long believed in jinn and harmful mythical creatures  
  _[fa]_ ← "در میان بسیاری اقوام از دیرباز باور به جن و موجودات افسانه‌ای آسیب‌رسان رواج داشته و برای احساس امنیت در قبال آن‌ها برخی افراد ورد و ذکرهایی را نوشته و با خود ه…"
- Some people write incantations and phrases to feel secure against these entities  
  _[fa]_ ← "در میان بسیاری اقوام از دیرباز باور به جن و موجودات افسانه‌ای آسیب‌رسان رواج داشته و برای احساس امنیت در قبال آن‌ها برخی افراد ورد و ذکرهایی را نوشته و با خود ه…"
- These written words and phrases are called 'ta'wiz' (amulets)  
  _[fa]_ ← "در میان بسیاری اقوام از دیرباز باور به جن و موجودات افسانه‌ای آسیب‌رسان رواج داشته و برای احساس امنیت در قبال آن‌ها برخی افراد ورد و ذکرهایی را نوشته و با خود ه…"
- Charity is something that wards off calamity from a person  
  _[fa]_ ← "۳ - چیزی که بلا را از آدمی دور گرداند، صدقه، قربانی» روش‌های دیگر بلاگردانی در ایران لال شیش؛ چوب بلاگردان، تیر آرش می‌باشد."
- Sacrifice is something that wards off calamity from a person  
  _[fa]_ ← "۳ - چیزی که بلا را از آدمی دور گرداند، صدقه، قربانی» روش‌های دیگر بلاگردانی در ایران لال شیش؛ چوب بلاگردان، تیر آرش می‌باشد."
- Other methods of warding off calamity in Iran include the 'lal shish'  
  _[fa]_ ← "۳ - چیزی که بلا را از آدمی دور گرداند، صدقه، قربانی» روش‌های دیگر بلاگردانی در ایران لال شیش؛ چوب بلاگردان، تیر آرش می‌باشد."
- The wood of the calamity-warder is known as the 'tir arash'  
  _[fa]_ ← "۳ - چیزی که بلا را از آدمی دور گرداند، صدقه، قربانی» روش‌های دیگر بلاگردانی در ایران لال شیش؛ چوب بلاگردان، تیر آرش می‌باشد."
- The text mentions the Blue Amulet of Dajana  
  _[fa]_ ← "حرز ابی دجانه حرز نهم حرز امام جواد حرز کبیر طلسم خمسه چشم‌زخم"
- The text mentions the Ninth Amulet  
  _[fa]_ ← "حرز ابی دجانه حرز نهم حرز امام جواد حرز کبیر طلسم خمسه چشم‌زخم"
- The text mentions the Amulet of Imam Javad  
  _[fa]_ ← "حرز ابی دجانه حرز نهم حرز امام جواد حرز کبیر طلسم خمسه چشم‌زخم"
- The text mentions the Great Amulet  
  _[fa]_ ← "حرز ابی دجانه حرز نهم حرز امام جواد حرز کبیر طلسم خمسه چشم‌زخم"
- The text mentions the Five-Eye Talisman  
  _[fa]_ ← "حرز ابی دجانه حرز نهم حرز امام جواد حرز کبیر طلسم خمسه چشم‌زخم"
- The text mentions the Eye-Talcing Talisman  
  _[fa]_ ← "حرز ابی دجانه حرز نهم حرز امام جواد حرز کبیر طلسم خمسه چشم‌زخم"
- A grigri is a form of magic  
  _[fr]_ ← "Apotropaïque, Talisman Magie Grigri Ésotérisme Superstition Boule de sorcière Fer à cheval Mojo (culture) Patte de lapin Hamsa Nazar boncuk Norigae Nœud sans fi…"
- The Nazar boncuk is an evil eye amulet  
  _[fr]_ ← "Apotropaïque, Talisman Magie Grigri Ésotérisme Superstition Boule de sorcière Fer à cheval Mojo (culture) Patte de lapin Hamsa Nazar boncuk Norigae Nœud sans fi…"
- A kamea (also spelled kamia) is an object  
  _[he]_ ← "קָמֵעַ (גם קָמִיעַ) הוא חפץ שמיוחסים לו כוחות סגוליים."
- Magical powers are attributed to a kamea  
  _[he]_ ← "קָמֵעַ (גם קָמִיעַ) הוא חפץ שמיוחסים לו כוחות סגוליים."
- A person places an amulet in their home  
  _[he]_ ← "את הקמע נושא אדם על גופו או מניח בביתו כדי להבטיח את שלומו, לשמירה מעין הרע או משדים ורוחות רעות."
- The purpose is to ensure safety and well-being  
  _[he]_ ← "את הקמע נושא אדם על גופו או מניח בביתו כדי להבטיח את שלומו, לשמירה מעין הרע או משדים ורוחות רעות."
- According to the author of the Aruch, the root 'Kame' means 'knot' in Aramaic  
  _[he]_ ← "השורש האטימולוגי של השם "קמע", לדעתו של בעל הערוך הוא משורש "קמע" שפירושו בארמית קשר, מכיוון שהיו עונדים אותו על הגוף; וכך מקובל בקרב חוקרי הלשון כיום."
- This meaning is derived from the fact that the item was worn on the body  
  _[he]_ ← "השורש האטימולוגי של השם "קמע", לדעתו של בעל הערוך הוא משורש "קמע" שפירושו בארמית קשר, מכיוון שהיו עונדים אותו על הגוף; וכך מקובל בקרב חוקרי הלשון כיום."
- This interpretation is accepted by modern linguists  
  _[he]_ ← "השורש האטימולוגי של השם "קמע", לדעתו של בעל הערוך הוא משורש "קמע" שפירושו בארמית קשר, מכיוון שהיו עונדים אותו על הגוף; וכך מקובל בקרב חוקרי הלשון כיום."
- Rabbi Eliyahu Bachor holds a specific view regarding the word 'qamea'  
  _[he]_ ← "לדעתו של רבי אליהו בחור, הוא מן המילה הארמית "קימעא", שפירושה "מעט", כיוון שהיו כותבים בקמע מילים מועטות."
- The purpose of these verses is to protect a home or business  
  _[he]_ ← "קמעות נוספים הם הכתב הערבי, שבו כותבים את פסוקים מהקוראן הנחשבים מקודשים שמגינים על הבית או בית העסק ומגנים עליו."
- Bedouins believe in the existence of evil eyes  
  _[he]_ ← "הבדווים מאמינים בקיום רוחות, שדים ועינא בישא."
- Bedouins attribute special importance to things that came from the mouth of the Prophet  
  _[he]_ ← "הבדווים מייחסים חשיבות מיוחדת לדברים שיצאו מפי הנביא: "הריפוי שלכם בשלושה: לגימה מהדבש, כוויה באש ופסוק מהקוראן", כנאמר בשפת העם "תמרה וג'מרה ואיה מן אל קוראן" …"
- The cure consists of three elements: drinking honey, burning with fire, and a verse from the Quran  
  _[he]_ ← "הבדווים מייחסים חשיבות מיוחדת לדברים שיצאו מפי הנביא: "הריפוי שלכם בשלושה: לגימה מהדבש, כוויה באש ופסוק מהקוראן", כנאמר בשפת העם "תמרה וג'מרה ואיה מן אל קוראן" …"
- This saying is expressed in the vernacular as 'Tamra wa Jamra wa Ayah min al-Quran'  
  _[he]_ ← "הבדווים מייחסים חשיבות מיוחדת לדברים שיצאו מפי הנביא: "הריפוי שלכם בשלושה: לגימה מהדבש, כוויה באש ופסוק מהקוראן", כנאמר בשפת העם "תמרה וג'מרה ואיה מן אל קוראן" …"
- Traditional Bedouins usually refer to dervishes  
  _[he]_ ← "הבדווי המסורתי בדרך כלל יפנה אל הדרווישים למצוא תרופה לסוג מסוים של מחלות ודאגות."
- They do this to find a remedy for specific types of illnesses and worries  
  _[he]_ ← "הבדווי המסורתי בדרך כלל יפנה אל הדרווישים למצוא תרופה לסוג מסוים של מחלות ודאגות."
- Some of these remedies are amulets  
  _[he]_ ← "חלק מתרופות אלה הן הקמיעות."
- Wearing an amulet on the body is one way to protect oneself from illness or witchcraft  
  _[he]_ ← "תליית קמיע על הגוף היא אחת הדרכים להישמר או להירפא ממחלה או כישוף."
- Wearing an amulet on the body is one way to heal from illness or witchcraft  
  _[he]_ ← "תליית קמיע על הגוף היא אחת הדרכים להישמר או להירפא ממחלה או כישוף."
- Blue beads were hung on the baby's head  
  _[he]_ ← "לדוגמה: תלית חרוזים בצבע תכלת על ראש התינוק."
- The beads are called 'qushash'  
  _[he]_ ← "החרוזים נקראים "קושאש" (המילה "קוש" פירושה לגרש את עין הרע, את המחלה או את מעשי הכישוף)."
- Adults wear amulets on their bodies  
  _[he]_ ← "המבוגרים תולים קמיעות על גופם, בפרט כאשר הם חשים מאוימים על ידי יריביהם."
- Adults are particularly likely to wear amulets when they feel threatened by their enemies  
  _[he]_ ← "המבוגרים תולים קמיעות על גופם, בפרט כאשר הם חשים מאוימים על ידי יריביהם."
- The amulet is placed under the pillow  
  _[he]_ ← "אם נושא הקמיע סובל ממחלה הנגרמת על ידי שדים מן האדמה, הקמיע יונח מתחת לכרית השינה."
- This action is taken if the wearer suffers from an illness caused by earth demons  
  _[he]_ ← "אם נושא הקמיע סובל ממחלה הנגרמת על ידי שדים מן האדמה, הקמיע יונח מתחת לכרית השינה."
- The term 'taweez' is also used in reference to other types of amulets  
  _[hi]_ ← "मुसलमानों द्वारा पहनी जाने वाली तवीज़ में कुरान और / या अन्य इस्लामी प्रार्थना का समावेश हैं। कुछ मुसलमानों तवीज़ को बुराई से बचाने के लिए पहना है। तावीज़ शब्द …"
- A taweez can be an engraving on metal  
  _[hi]_ ← "मुसलमानों द्वारा पहनी जाने वाली तवीज़ में कुरान और / या अन्य इस्लामी प्रार्थना का समावेश हैं। कुछ मुसलमानों तवीज़ को बुराई से बचाने के लिए पहना है। तावीज़ शब्द …"
- A taweez can be a framed pair  
  _[hi]_ ← "मुसलमानों द्वारा पहनी जाने वाली तवीज़ में कुरान और / या अन्य इस्लामी प्रार्थना का समावेश हैं। कुछ मुसलमानों तवीज़ को बुराई से बचाने के लिए पहना है। तावीज़ शब्द …"
- The word 'taweez' is used in Urdu and Hindi  
  _[hi]_ ← "उर्दू और हिंदी में इस्तेमाल होने वाला शब्द तावीज़ अरबी से आता है। अरबी शब्द tawwīdh, जिसका अर्थ है "ताबीज" या "आकर्षण" क्रिया awwadha से बना है, जिसका अर्थ है "…"
- The word 'taweez' originates from Arabic  
  _[hi]_ ← "उर्दू और हिंदी में इस्तेमाल होने वाला शब्द तावीज़ अरबी से आता है। अरबी शब्द tawwīdh, जिसका अर्थ है "ताबीज" या "आकर्षण" क्रिया awwadha से बना है, जिसका अर्थ है "…"
- The Arabic word for 'taweez' is 'tawwīdh'  
  _[hi]_ ← "उर्दू और हिंदी में इस्तेमाल होने वाला शब्द तावीज़ अरबी से आता है। अरबी शब्द tawwīdh, जिसका अर्थ है "ताबीज" या "आकर्षण" क्रिया awwadha से बना है, जिसका अर्थ है "…"
- 'Tawwīdh' means 'taweez' or 'attraction'  
  _[hi]_ ← "उर्दू और हिंदी में इस्तेमाल होने वाला शब्द तावीज़ अरबी से आता है। अरबी शब्द tawwīdh, जिसका अर्थ है "ताबीज" या "आकर्षण" क्रिया awwadha से बना है, जिसका अर्थ है "…"
- The verb 'tawwīdh' is derived from the root 'awwadha'  
  _[hi]_ ← "उर्दू और हिंदी में इस्तेमाल होने वाला शब्द तावीज़ अरबी से आता है। अरबी शब्द tawwīdh, जिसका अर्थ है "ताबीज" या "आकर्षण" क्रिया awwadha से बना है, जिसका अर्थ है "…"
- 'Awwadha' means 'to strengthen someone with a taweez or ash'  
  _[hi]_ ← "उर्दू और हिंदी में इस्तेमाल होने वाला शब्द तावीज़ अरबी से आता है। अरबी शब्द tawwīdh, जिसका अर्थ है "ताबीज" या "आकर्षण" क्रिया awwadha से बना है, जिसका अर्थ है "…"
- The word 'amulet' is written as 'Tilasim' in Arabic  
  _[hi]_ ← "अंग्रेज़ी - ऐम्युलॅट (amulet) अरबी: तिलासिम () फ़ारसी - तावीज़ ()"
- The word 'amulet' is written as 'Taweez' in Persian  
  _[hi]_ ← "अंग्रेज़ी - ऐम्युलॅट (amulet) अरबी: तिलासिम () फ़ारसी - तावीज़ ()"
- Seeking protection and healing from entities other than Allah is prohibited  
  _[id]_ ← "Hal ini dikarenakan perbuatan tersebut merupakan bentuk meminta perlindungan dan penyembuhan kepada selain Allah."
- This practice was commonly performed by the Jahiliyah (pagans)  
  _[id]_ ← "Selain itu perbuatan ini juga perbuatan yang biasa dilakukan kaum Jahiliyah (paganis) yang terlarang untuk dikuti."
- The Jahiliyah (pagans) are forbidden to be imitated  
  _[id]_ ← "Selain itu perbuatan ini juga perbuatan yang biasa dilakukan kaum Jahiliyah (paganis) yang terlarang untuk dikuti."
- It has become common to place paper with verses from the Quran instead of soil in talismans  
  _[kk]_ ← "Ислам дінінің әсерімен, тұмарға туған жердің топырағын салу ығыстырылап, орнына Құран аяттары жазылған қағаз салу белең алған."
- Tumars are usually written by religious scholars  
  _[kk]_ ← "Тұмарды әдетте діни ғұламаларға жаздырып алып, былғарымен қаптап, бойға тағып жүреді."
- The written text is covered with leather  
  _[kk]_ ← "Тұмарды әдетте діни ғұламаларға жаздырып алып, былғарымен қаптап, бойға тағып жүреді."
- People wear the tumar on their body  
  _[kk]_ ← "Тұмарды әдетте діни ғұламаларға жаздырып алып, былғарымен қаптап, бойға тағып жүреді."
- Tilla tumar is a type of tumar  
  _[kk]_ ← "Тіллә тұмар - алтынмен қапталған тұмардың бір түрі."
- Tilla tumar is covered with gold  
  _[kk]_ ← "Тіллә тұмар - алтынмен қапталған тұмардың бір түрі."
- Amulets written on paper are also called 'p'ajik'  
  _[ko]_ ← "종이에 쓴 주구를 특히 주부 또는 부적이라고 한다."
- Arabs often draw the symbol of an eye above doors  
  _[lt]_ ← "Arabai virš durų dažnai piešia akies simbolį."
- Small leather pouches serve as amulets for Arab peoples  
  _[lv]_ ← "Kā piemēram, arābu tautām par amuletiem kalpot nelielas ādas somiņas, kurās ir iešūts neliela papīra gabaliņš, uz kura ir uzrakstīta kāda Korāna sūra vai uzzīmē…"
- These pouches contain a small piece of paper sewn inside  
  _[lv]_ ← "Kā piemēram, arābu tautām par amuletiem kalpot nelielas ādas somiņas, kurās ir iešūts neliela papīra gabaliņš, uz kura ir uzrakstīta kāda Korāna sūra vai uzzīmē…"
- The paper has a Quranic sura written on it or a magical symbol drawn on it  
  _[lv]_ ← "Kā piemēram, arābu tautām par amuletiem kalpot nelielas ādas somiņas, kurās ir iešūts neliela papīra gabaliņš, uz kura ir uzrakstīta kāda Korāna sūra vai uzzīmē…"
- The amulet includes the 'Eye of Fatima' (nazar)  
  _[pl]_ ← "talizman palladion amulety starożytnego Egiptu "oko Fatimy" (nazar) oraz "ręka Fatimy" (chamsa) krzyż św. Benedykta, Cudowny Medalik"
- Amulets among Arabs are small leather pouches  
  _[ru]_ ← "У арабов амулеты — это маленькие кожаные сумки с вшитой бумагой, на которую написана сура Корана или магический знак."
- The pouches contain paper sewn inside them  
  _[ru]_ ← "У арабов амулеты — это маленькие кожаные сумки с вшитой бумагой, на которую написана сура Корана или магический знак."
- The paper has a Quranic sura written on it or a magical symbol  
  _[ru]_ ← "У арабов амулеты — это маленькие кожаные сумки с вшитой бумагой, на которую написана сура Корана или магический знак."
- Maghreb states use the Hand of Fatima as an amulet  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- Turks use the Nazar or 'Eye of Fatima' (a stone against the evil eye) as an amulet  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- The type is four-sided  
  _[tr]_ ← "Dört köşeli olan ve hamaylı (hamail) adıyla bilinen türü bütün İslâm dünyasında yaygın biçimde kullanılmaktadır."
- The type is known as hamaylı (hamail)  
  _[tr]_ ← "Dört köşeli olan ve hamaylı (hamail) adıyla bilinen türü bütün İslâm dünyasında yaygın biçimde kullanılmaktadır."
- This type is widely used throughout the Islamic world  
  _[tr]_ ← "Dört köşeli olan ve hamaylı (hamail) adıyla bilinen türü bütün İslâm dünyasında yaygın biçimde kullanılmaktadır."
- Names of angels are written or drawn  
  _[tr]_ ← "Bunun dışında meleklerin, peygamberlerin, hatta efsanevi kişilerin adları ve anlaşılmaz tılsımlı sözler, simgeler, yıldız işaretleri, rakamlar, işaretler, insan…"
- Names of legendary figures are written or drawn  
  _[tr]_ ← "Bunun dışında meleklerin, peygamberlerin, hatta efsanevi kişilerin adları ve anlaşılmaz tılsımlı sözler, simgeler, yıldız işaretleri, rakamlar, işaretler, insan…"
- Incomprehensible magical words are written or drawn  
  _[tr]_ ← "Bunun dışında meleklerin, peygamberlerin, hatta efsanevi kişilerin adları ve anlaşılmaz tılsımlı sözler, simgeler, yıldız işaretleri, rakamlar, işaretler, insan…"
- The word 'muska' is derived from the Arabic word 'nusha'  
  _[tr]_ ← "Muska sözcüğü Arapça "nasaha" (kopyaladı, temize çekti) fiilinden adlaşmış olan "nüsha"dan türemiştir."
- 'Nusha' is a noun form of the Arabic verb 'nasaha'  
  _[tr]_ ← "Muska sözcüğü Arapça "nasaha" (kopyaladı, temize çekti) fiilinden adlaşmış olan "nüsha"dan türemiştir."
- The Arabic verb 'nasaha' means 'copied' or 'cleaned up'  
  _[tr]_ ← "Muska sözcüğü Arapça "nasaha" (kopyaladı, temize çekti) fiilinden adlaşmış olan "nüsha"dan türemiştir."
- Tumar (or Tomar) is an object  
  _[tr]_ ← "Tumar, ya da Tomar, ise Türk, Altay ve Orta Asya Türki toplulukları halk kültüründe ve halk inancında kullandıkları muska bir eşdeğeri eşyadır."
- Tumar serves as a talisman equivalent  
  _[tr]_ ← "Tumar, ya da Tomar, ise Türk, Altay ve Orta Asya Türki toplulukları halk kültüründe ve halk inancında kullandıkları muska bir eşdeğeri eşyadır."
- Tumar is used in the folk culture of Turkish, Altay, and Central Asian Turkic communities  
  _[tr]_ ← "Tumar, ya da Tomar, ise Türk, Altay ve Orta Asya Türki toplulukları halk kültüründe ve halk inancında kullandıkları muska bir eşdeğeri eşyadır."
- Tumar is used in the folk beliefs of Turkish, Altay, and Central Asian Turkic communities  
  _[tr]_ ← "Tumar, ya da Tomar, ise Türk, Altay ve Orta Asya Türki toplulukları halk kültüründe ve halk inancında kullandıkları muska bir eşdeğeri eşyadır."
- The word 'Tumar' or 'Tomar' derives from the root 'Tom' or 'Tum'  
  _[tr]_ ← "Tom veya Tum kökünden türemiştir."
- The word refers to rolled paper  
  _[tr]_ ← "Kıvrılmış kâğıt demektir."
- The meanings of 'to roll' and 'to curl' exist in all dialects of the Tungus language  
  _[tr]_ ← "Yuvarlamak, kıvırmak anlamları Türkçe, Tunguzca ve Moğolca'nın tüm lehçelerinde mevcuttur."
- The meanings of 'to roll' and 'to curl' exist in all dialects of the Mongolian language  
  _[tr]_ ← "Yuvarlamak, kıvırmak anlamları Türkçe, Tunguzca ve Moğolca'nın tüm lehçelerinde mevcuttur."
- The word 'Tom' or 'Yom' expresses sanctity  
  _[tr]_ ← "Ayrıca Tom veya Yom sözcüğü kutsallık ve talih ifade eder."
- The word 'Tom' or 'Yom' expresses luck  
  _[tr]_ ← "Ayrıca Tom veya Yom sözcüğü kutsallık ve talih ifade eder."
- In the Tungus language, the words Tomka or Tomko mean to roll up or bend  
  _[tr]_ ← "Tunguzcada Tomka veya Tomko, Moğolcada Tom, Tomu veya Tomo sözcükleri yuvarlamak, bükmek manalarını ihtiva eder."
- In the Mongolian language, the words Tom, Tomu, or Tomo mean to roll up or bend  
  _[tr]_ ← "Tunguzcada Tomka veya Tomko, Moğolcada Tom, Tomu veya Tomo sözcükleri yuvarlamak, bükmek manalarını ihtiva eder."
- The text refers to an object or concept named 'Tılsım Cevşen'  
  _[tr]_ ← "Tılsım Cevşen"

### Materiality of Islamic amulets
- Talismans are placed inside silver coverings  
  _[az]_ ← "Dəri, meşin, gümüş və qızıl örtüklər içinə qoyularaq boyuna asılır ya da qola ilişər."
- Talismans are placed inside gold coverings  
  _[az]_ ← "Dəri, meşin, gümüş və qızıl örtüklər içinə qoyularaq boyuna asılır ya da qola ilişər."
- Covered talismans are hung around the neck  
  _[az]_ ← "Dəri, meşin, gümüş və qızıl örtüklər içinə qoyularaq boyuna asılır ya da qola ilişər."
- Covered talismans are attached to the arm  
  _[az]_ ← "Dəri, meşin, gümüş və qızıl örtüklər içinə qoyularaq boyuna asılır ya da qola ilişər."
- Surahs are written on muskals  
  _[az]_ ← "Muskalara surə, ayə, hədis və ya bir dua yaxud da Allahın adları yazılar."
- Ayats are written on muskals  
  _[az]_ ← "Muskalara surə, ayə, hədis və ya bir dua yaxud da Allahın adları yazılar."
- Hadiths are written on muskals  
  _[az]_ ← "Muskalara surə, ayə, hədis və ya bir dua yaxud da Allahın adları yazılar."
- A prayer is written on muskals  
  _[az]_ ← "Muskalara surə, ayə, hədis və ya bir dua yaxud da Allahın adları yazılar."
- The names of Allah are written on muskals  
  _[az]_ ← "Muskalara surə, ayə, hədis və ya bir dua yaxud da Allahın adları yazılar."
- Numbers are written or drawn on the talisman  
  _[az]_ ← "Bundan başqa mələklərin, peyğəmbərlərin, hətta əfsanəvi şəxslərin adları və aydın olmaz sehrli sözlər, işarələr, ulduz işarələri, rəqəmlər, insan və heyvan şəki…"
- Strange letter shapes are written or drawn on the talisman  
  _[az]_ ← "Bundan başqa mələklərin, peyğəmbərlərin, hətta əfsanəvi şəxslərin adları və aydın olmaz sehrli sözlər, işarələr, ulduz işarələri, rəqəmlər, insan və heyvan şəki…"
- Colored metals have an amulet-like positive effect  
  _[de]_ ← "Eine amulettartige positive Wirkung entfalten im Volksglauben Buntmetalle, besonders Kupfer und Messing."
- Copper is particularly effective as a colored metal in this context  
  _[de]_ ← "Eine amulettartige positive Wirkung entfalten im Volksglauben Buntmetalle, besonders Kupfer und Messing."
- Brass is particularly effective as a colored metal in this context  
  _[de]_ ← "Eine amulettartige positive Wirkung entfalten im Volksglauben Buntmetalle, besonders Kupfer und Messing."
- The paper or parchment contains some blessing written on it  
  _[he]_ ← "לרוב יהיה הקמע פיסת נייר או קלף שעליה כתובה ברכה כלשהי, צירופי אותיות של פסוקים, שמות מלאכים או שמות קדושים אחרים."
- The paper or parchment contains combinations of letters from verses  
  _[he]_ ← "לרוב יהיה הקמע פיסת נייר או קלף שעליה כתובה ברכה כלשהי, צירופי אותיות של פסוקים, שמות מלאכים או שמות קדושים אחרים."
- The paper or parchment contains names of angels  
  _[he]_ ← "לרוב יהיה הקמע פיסת נייר או קלף שעליה כתובה ברכה כלשהי, צירופי אותיות של פסוקים, שמות מלאכים או שמות קדושים אחרים."
- The paper or parchment contains other holy names  
  _[he]_ ← "לרוב יהיה הקמע פיסת נייר או קלף שעליה כתובה ברכה כלשהי, צירופי אותיות של פסוקים, שמות מלאכים או שמות קדושים אחרים."
- Colors and symbols acquired mystical significance in Islamic belief alongside the hamza  
  _[he]_ ← "לצד החמסה גם צבעים וסמלים מסיומים קיבלו נפח מיסטי באמונה המוסלמית כך למשל הצבע הכחול נחשב לקמע המגן מפני עין רע ובתרבות הטורקית נוהגים להשתמש "בנאזירי" שהם עיני…"
- The amulet shield is written in ink on paper  
  _[he]_ ← "מגן הקמיע נכתב בדיו על נייר, ולאחר מכן הוא מקופל לצורת משולש ונעטף בבד או בעור ונתלה על הגוף."
- The amulet shield is folded into a triangle shape  
  _[he]_ ← "מגן הקמיע נכתב בדיו על נייר, ולאחר מכן הוא מקופל לצורת משולש ונעטף בבד או בעור ונתלה על הגוף."
- The amulet shield is wrapped in cloth or leather  
  _[he]_ ← "מגן הקמיע נכתב בדיו על נייר, ולאחר מכן הוא מקופל לצורת משולש ונעטף בבד או בעור ונתלה על הגוף."
- The amulet shield is hung on the body  
  _[he]_ ← "מגן הקמיע נכתב בדיו על נייר, ולאחר מכן הוא מקופל לצורת משולש ונעטף בבד או בעור ונתלה על הגוף."
- Some amulets contain numbers  
  _[he]_ ← "חלק מהקמיעות מכיל צירופי אותיות, מילים, סמלים, מספרים, פסוקים מהקוראן וכדומה, אשר רק כותב הקמיעות יודע את מובנם."
- Some amulets contain combinations of letters  
  _[he]_ ← "חלק מהקמיעות מכיל צירופי אותיות, מילים, סמלים, מספרים, פסוקים מהקוראן וכדומה, אשר רק כותב הקמיעות יודע את מובנם."
- Only the amulet writer knows the meaning of these contents  
  _[he]_ ← "חלק מהקמיעות מכיל צירופי אותיות, מילים, סמלים, מספרים, פסוקים מהקוראן וכדומה, אשר רק כותב הקמיעות יודע את מובנם."
- The amulet could be made using cut-out paper or collage  
  _[ro]_ ← "decupaj sau colaj (ca și obiectul "șeviti") și conținea un text caligrafiat, ornat dacă permitea suportul cu elemente executate în tuș și colorate."
- The amulet contained calligraphed text  
  _[ro]_ ← "decupaj sau colaj (ca și obiectul "șeviti") și conținea un text caligrafiat, ornat dacă permitea suportul cu elemente executate în tuș și colorate."
- The design included elements executed in ink  
  _[ro]_ ← "decupaj sau colaj (ca și obiectul "șeviti") și conținea un text caligrafiat, ornat dacă permitea suportul cu elemente executate în tuș și colorate."
- The design included colored elements  
  _[ro]_ ← "decupaj sau colaj (ca și obiectul "șeviti") și conținea un text caligrafiat, ornat dacă permitea suportul cu elemente executate în tuș și colorate."
- Colored metals have a positive effect similar to amulets  
  _[ru]_ ← "Подобное амулету положительное действие имеют в народных верованиях цветные металлы, особенно медь и латунь."
- Copper is particularly associated with this effect  
  _[ru]_ ← "Подобное амулету положительное действие имеют в народных верованиях цветные металлы, особенно медь и латунь."
- Brass is particularly associated with this effect  
  _[ru]_ ← "Подобное амулету положительное действие имеют в народных верованиях цветные металлы, особенно медь и латунь."
- Numbers are written or drawn  
  _[tr]_ ← "Bunun dışında meleklerin, peygamberlerin, hatta efsanevi kişilerin adları ve anlaşılmaz tılsımlı sözler, simgeler, yıldız işaretleri, rakamlar, işaretler, insan…"
- Strange letter shapes are written or drawn  
  _[tr]_ ← "Bunun dışında meleklerin, peygamberlerin, hatta efsanevi kişilerin adları ve anlaşılmaz tılsımlı sözler, simgeler, yıldız işaretleri, rakamlar, işaretler, insan…"
- These amulets are typically wrapped in fabrics  
  _[uk]_ ← "Як правило загортаються в тканини, шкіру, вкладаються в футляри і носяться на тілі."
- These amulets are typically wrapped in leather  
  _[uk]_ ← "Як правило загортаються в тканини, шкіру, вкладаються в футляри і носяться на тілі."
- These amulets are typically placed in cases  
  _[uk]_ ← "Як правило загортаються в тканини, шкіру, вкладаються в футляри і носяться на тілі."

### China, Korea, Japan
- The oldest Chinese literature mentions amulets  
  _[cs]_ ← "O amuletech (ve skutečnosti šlo o talismany) se zmiňuje už nejstarší čínská literatura."
- In later times, amulets were often made from paper  
  _[cs]_ ← "Vyráběly se z různých materiálů, v pozdější době často z papíru."
- The amulets bore a message  
  _[cs]_ ← "Stálo na nich poselství zlým duchům s prosbou, aby nositeli amuletu neubližovali."
- The message was directed to evil spirits  
  _[cs]_ ← "Stálo na nich poselství zlým duchům s prosbou, aby nositeli amuletu neubližovali."
- The message requested that the spirits not harm the wearer of the amulet  
  _[cs]_ ← "Stálo na nich poselství zlým duchům s prosbou, aby nositeli amuletu neubližovali."
- They used a special script  
  _[cs]_ ← "Proto používali zvláštní písmo."
- The spirit script bears a certain resemblance to Chinese characters  
  _[cs]_ ← "Znakové písmo duchů má jistou podobnost s čínským písmem."
- Taoists know the Spirit Script perfectly  
  _[cs]_ ← "Dokonale ho znají taoisté."
- The spirit script is very ancient  
  _[cs]_ ← "Písmo duchů je velmi staré."
- The author of the book Pao-pchu-c is Ke Chung  
  _[cs]_ ← "V knize Pao-pchu-c´ od Ke Chunga (281–361 po Kr.) je obsažen celý jeho slovník."
- Ke Chung lived between 281 and 361 AD  
  _[cs]_ ← "V knize Pao-pchu-c´ od Ke Chunga (281–361 po Kr.) je obsažen celý jeho slovník."
- The Chinese used paper from invalid calendars as amulets  
  _[cs]_ ← "Protože Číňané považovali kalendář za nesmírně účinný a nepostradatelný, používali papír neplatných kalendářů jako amulet."
- The Chinese considered the calendar to be extremely effective  
  _[cs]_ ← "Protože Číňané považovali kalendář za nesmírně účinný a nepostradatelný, používali papír neplatných kalendářů jako amulet."
- The Chinese considered the calendar to be indispensable  
  _[cs]_ ← "Protože Číňané považovali kalendář za nesmírně účinný a nepostradatelný, používali papír neplatných kalendářů jako amulet."
- Old calendars were hung on the walls of pigsties  
  _[cs]_ ← "Staré kalendáře se věšely na zdi prasečích chlívků."
- Old calendars were burned  
  _[cs]_ ← "A tyto staré kalendáře také byly spalovány a přimíchávány zvířatům do krmení proti morovým ranám."
- The ash from the burned calendars was mixed into animal feed  
  _[cs]_ ← "A tyto staré kalendáře také byly spalovány a přimíchávány zvířatům do krmení proti morovým ranám."
- There is a very famous amulet associated with Taoism  
  _[cs]_ ← "S taoismem je spojený velmi známý amulet zobrazující mužský a ženský princip jin a jang."
- This amulet depicts the male and female principles of Yin and Yang  
  _[cs]_ ← "S taoismem je spojený velmi známý amulet zobrazující mužský a ženský princip jin a jang."
- Chinese people used perforated coins as amulets  
  _[de]_ ← "Alten Ägyptern: Skarabäus, Knoten, Horusauge Assyrern: Täfelchen mit Beschwörungsformeln Chinesen: Lochmünzen, Zauberformeln ("fu") in Geheimschrift Christen: K…"
- Chinese people used magic formulas called 'fu' written in secret script  
  _[de]_ ← "Alten Ägyptern: Skarabäus, Knoten, Horusauge Assyrern: Täfelchen mit Beschwörungsformeln Chinesen: Lochmünzen, Zauberformeln ("fu") in Geheimschrift Christen: K…"
- The Norigae is a Korean charm  
  _[fr]_ ← "Apotropaïque, Talisman Magie Grigri Ésotérisme Superstition Boule de sorcière Fer à cheval Mojo (culture) Patte de lapin Hamsa Nazar boncuk Norigae Nœud sans fi…"
- An ancient Chinese tradition teaches how to capture a live cricket  
  _[it]_ ← "Un'antica tradizione cinese insegna a catturare un grillo vivo e tenerlo in una scatola di vimini per attirare la buona sorte (questa tradizione si estende anch…"
- This practice is done to attract good luck  
  _[it]_ ← "Un'antica tradizione cinese insegna a catturare un grillo vivo e tenerlo in una scatola di vimini per attirare la buona sorte (questa tradizione si estende anch…"
- In China, people can scatter coins on the floor  
  _[it]_ ← "Sempre in Cina si possono spargere le monete sul pavimento per "attirare" il denaro;"
- In Japan, Shinto temples prepare small colorful cloth bags  
  _[it]_ ← "In Giappone i templi shintoisti preparano piccoli sacchetti di stoffa colorata chiamati omamori, dedicati alla protezione di molti aspetti della vita (amore, de…"

  _[it]_ ← "In Giappone i templi shintoisti preparano piccoli sacchetti di stoffa colorata chiamati omamori, dedicati alla protezione di molti aspetti della vita (amore, de…"
- Omamori are dedicated to protecting many aspects of life  
  _[it]_ ← "In Giappone i templi shintoisti preparano piccoli sacchetti di stoffa colorata chiamati omamori, dedicati alla protezione di molti aspetti della vita (amore, de…"
- Protected aspects include love, money, and studies  
  _[it]_ ← "In Giappone i templi shintoisti preparano piccoli sacchetti di stoffa colorata chiamati omamori, dedicati alla protezione di molti aspetti della vita (amore, de…"
- Sarubobo are found in the city of Takayama  
  _[it]_ ← "Oltre a questo, nella città di Takayama sono diffusi i sarubobo."
- Chinese people used coins with holes as amulets  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- Chinese people used magical formulas ('fu') in secret script as amulets  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- Japanese people use Maneki-neko as an amulet  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- Fulu is believed to protect against evil spirits  
  _[sl]_ ← "Na Kitajskem so taoistični strokovnjaki imenovali fulu poseben slog kaligrafije, za katerega pravijo da bi lahko zaščitila pred zli duhovi."
- This Japanese amulet is called ofuda  
  _[sl]_ ← "Enakovredni tip amuleta na Japonskem se imenuje ofuda."
- Yếm thắng vật, yếm thắng thuật, or trấn yểm are practices involving earth binding with charms  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng vật (), yếm thắng thuật () hay trấn yểm () là việc yểm đất bằng bùa chú để phá đi mạch nước tốt trong đất, ngăn chặn vượng khí và gia tăng linh khí ha…"
- The purpose of these practices is to break good water veins in the ground  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng vật (), yếm thắng thuật () hay trấn yểm () là việc yểm đất bằng bùa chú để phá đi mạch nước tốt trong đất, ngăn chặn vượng khí và gia tăng linh khí ha…"
- The purpose includes blocking prosperous energy (vượng khí)  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng vật (), yếm thắng thuật () hay trấn yểm () là việc yểm đất bằng bùa chú để phá đi mạch nước tốt trong đất, ngăn chặn vượng khí và gia tăng linh khí ha…"
- The purpose includes increasing spiritual energy (linh khí)  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng vật (), yếm thắng thuật () hay trấn yểm () là việc yểm đất bằng bùa chú để phá đi mạch nước tốt trong đất, ngăn chặn vượng khí và gia tăng linh khí ha…"
- These practices may also involve using mantras to achieve better goals for a person  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng vật (), yếm thắng thuật () hay trấn yểm () là việc yểm đất bằng bùa chú để phá đi mạch nước tốt trong đất, ngăn chặn vượng khí và gia tăng linh khí ha…"
- Objects used include metal items, bone, charms, and evil spirits  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng vật (), yếm thắng thuật () hay trấn yểm () là việc yểm đất bằng bùa chú để phá đi mạch nước tốt trong đất, ngăn chặn vượng khí và gia tăng linh khí ha…"
- The objects are arranged according to the will of the practitioner  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng vật (), yếm thắng thuật () hay trấn yểm () là việc yểm đất bằng bùa chú để phá đi mạch nước tốt trong đất, ngăn chặn vượng khí và gia tăng linh khí ha…"
- Yem thang is an abbreviation for the phrase yem nhi thang chi  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng là viết tắt của từ yếm nhi thắng chi (, nghĩa đen là "tuy đầy đủ (yếm) nhưng vẫn muốn hơn/ được lợi (thắng)"."
- The literal meaning of yem nhi thang chi is 'although full (yem) but still wants more/ gains advantage (thang)'  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng là viết tắt của từ yếm nhi thắng chi (, nghĩa đen là "tuy đầy đủ (yếm) nhưng vẫn muốn hơn/ được lợi (thắng)"."
- Yem thang is also understood as tran yem (or tran yem)  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng còn được hiểu là trấn yểm ( hoặc trấn yếm ) trong đó "trấn" nghĩa đen là đè xuống và nghĩa rộng là áp chế, đàn áp, áp phục, canh giữ; "yểm" là giấu đi…"
- The term 'tran' literally means to press down  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng còn được hiểu là trấn yểm ( hoặc trấn yếm ) trong đó "trấn" nghĩa đen là đè xuống và nghĩa rộng là áp chế, đàn áp, áp phục, canh giữ; "yểm" là giấu đi…"
- The term 'tran' broadly means to suppress, oppress, subdue, or guard  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng còn được hiểu là trấn yểm ( hoặc trấn yếm ) trong đó "trấn" nghĩa đen là đè xuống và nghĩa rộng là áp chế, đàn áp, áp phục, canh giữ; "yểm" là giấu đi…"
- The term 'yem' means to hide away  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng còn được hiểu là trấn yểm ( hoặc trấn yếm ) trong đó "trấn" nghĩa đen là đè xuống và nghĩa rộng là áp chế, đàn áp, áp phục, canh giữ; "yểm" là giấu đi…"
- The literal meaning of 'yem' is to make an object unable to develop by being suppressed or hidden  
  _[vi]_ ← "Yếm thắng còn được hiểu là trấn yểm ( hoặc trấn yếm ) trong đó "trấn" nghĩa đen là đè xuống và nghĩa rộng là áp chế, đàn áp, áp phục, canh giữ; "yểm" là giấu đi…"
- Talismans are usually drawn by people with specific abilities  
  _[vi]_ ← "Một loại hình trấn yểm phổ biến là dùng phù lục thường do những người có công năng vẽ ra để dùng vào một mục đích cụ thể như trấn trạch, cầu an trừ tà, cầu tài …"
- Talismans are used for specific purposes such as stabilizing a property, seeking peace, driving out evil spirits, attracting wealth, or curing diseases  
  _[vi]_ ← "Một loại hình trấn yểm phổ biến là dùng phù lục thường do những người có công năng vẽ ra để dùng vào một mục đích cụ thể như trấn trạch, cầu an trừ tà, cầu tài …"
- Common belief holds that the power and durability of charms depend on the ability of the person who drew them  
  _[vi]_ ← "Một loại hình trấn yểm phổ biến là dùng phù lục thường do những người có công năng vẽ ra để dùng vào một mục đích cụ thể như trấn trạch, cầu an trừ tà, cầu tài …"
- Bagua Mirror is an example of a warding object  
  _[zh]_ ← "鎮五營(或稱安五營) 八卦鏡 風獅爺 風雞 (厭勝物) 獸面瓦(鬼瓦) 石敢當 劍獅"

### Ancient Egypt
- This practice was done to protect against plague  
  _[cs]_ ← "A tyto staré kalendáře také byly spalovány a přimíchávány zvířatům do krmení proti morovým ranám."
- The scarab beetle is also known as the sacred dung beetle  
  _[cs]_ ← "V Egyptě byl za posvátný amulet pokládán skarabeus (neboli brouk vrboun posvátný)."
- The scarab was considered a symbol of reincarnation  
  _[cs]_ ← "Tento byl považován za symbol reinkarnace a jeho podoba byla často vyrývána do kamenů."
- The image of the scarab was often carved into stones  
  _[cs]_ ← "Tento byl považován za symbol reinkarnace a jeho podoba byla často vyrývána do kamenů."
- The Egyptians called the scarab 'cheprer'  
  _[cs]_ ← "Egypťané ho nazývali cheprer a jeho znak znamená v hieroglyfech být, stát se."
- The symbol of the scarab means 'to be' in hieroglyphs  
  _[cs]_ ← "Egypťané ho nazývali cheprer a jeho znak znamená v hieroglyfech být, stát se."
- The symbol of the scarab means 'to become' in hieroglyphs  
  _[cs]_ ← "Egypťané ho nazývali cheprer a jeho znak znamená v hieroglyfech být, stát se."
- Scarab beetles with wings were considered exceptionally effective amulets  
  _[cs]_ ← "Za mimořádně účinné amulety byli považováni skarabeové s křídly."
- They were placed at the heart location of the mummies  
  _[cs]_ ← "Tito byli kladeni mumiím na místo srdce coby jejich ochránci u soudu mrtvých."
- They served as protectors for the mummies  
  _[cs]_ ← "Tito byli kladeni mumiím na místo srdce coby jejich ochránci u soudu mrtvých."
- Their role was to protect them in the judgment of the dead  
  _[cs]_ ← "Tito byli kladeni mumiím na místo srdce coby jejich ochránci u soudu mrtvých."
- The protective words came from the Book of the Dead  
  _[cs]_ ← "Na jejich spodní straně byla vyryta ochranná slova z Knihy mrtvých."
- Protective words were carved on the underside  
  _[cs]_ ← "Na jejich spodní straně byla vyryta ochranná slova z Knihy mrtvých."
- Ancient Egyptians used knots as amulets  
  _[de]_ ← "Alten Ägyptern: Skarabäus, Knoten, Horusauge Assyrern: Täfelchen mit Beschwörungsformeln Chinesen: Lochmünzen, Zauberformeln ("fu") in Geheimschrift Christen: K…"
- An scarab is a religious fetish  
  _[es]_ ← "Escapulario Fetichismo religioso Nazar Ojo apotropaico Grisgrís Pata de conejo Trébol de cuatro hojas Herradura Cornicello Escarabeo Jamsa Pentáculo Ojo Huichol…"
- Udyat is a religious fetish  
  _[es]_ ← "Escapulario Fetichismo religioso Nazar Ojo apotropaico Grisgrís Pata de conejo Trébol de cuatro hojas Herradura Cornicello Escarabeo Jamsa Pentáculo Ojo Huichol…"
- Ancient Egyptians wore amulets on all occasions  
  _[fr]_ ← "Bien qu'elles aient du succès aux quatre coins du monde, on associe souvent les amulettes aux anciens Égyptiens, qui en portaient en toute occasion, jusque dans…"
- It was common to bury a mummy with dozens of scarab-shaped amulets  
  _[fr]_ ← "Il était courant d'enterrer une momie avec des dizaines d'amulettes en forme de scarabée."
- The small stone figurines were amulets  
  _[fr]_ ← "Ces petites figurines de pierre devaient empêcher l'âme du défunt d'être dévorée par Ammout."
- The purpose of the amulets was to protect the soul of the deceased  
  _[fr]_ ← "Ces petites figurines de pierre devaient empêcher l'âme du défunt d'être dévorée par Ammout."
- The amulets were intended to prevent Ammut from devouring the soul  
  _[fr]_ ← "Ces petites figurines de pierre devaient empêcher l'âme du défunt d'être dévorée par Ammout."
- The number of scarab amulets a deceased person took with them to their tomb was related to their importance in life  
  _[fr]_ ← "Il semblerait que, plus le défunt avait été un personnage important de son vivant, plus il emportait d'amulettes-scarabées avec lui dans sa tombe."
- More important individuals carried more scarab amulets to their tombs  
  _[fr]_ ← "Il semblerait que, plus le défunt avait été un personnage important de son vivant, plus il emportait d'amulettes-scarabées avec lui dans sa tombe."
- These amulets protected against death  
  _[fr]_ ← "Mais les vivants aussi savaient s'entourer : les amulettes appelées ânkh (un hiéroglyphe symbolisant la vie) et oudjat (ou œil d'Horus) protégeaient de la mort,…"
- The ankh symbol is a hieroglyph representing life  
  _[fr]_ ← "Mais les vivants aussi savaient s'entourer : les amulettes appelées ânkh (un hiéroglyphe symbolisant la vie) et oudjat (ou œil d'Horus) protégeaient de la mort,…"
- Paintings depicting the eye of Horus were found  
  _[he]_ ← "ציורים בדמות עינו של הורוס נמצאו כבר על קברים קדומים המצרים, לצידם של מומיות."
- These paintings were found on ancient Egyptian tombs  
  _[he]_ ← "ציורים בדמות עינו של הורוס נמצאו כבר על קברים קדומים המצרים, לצידם של מומיות."
- The paintings were found alongside mummies  
  _[he]_ ← "ציורים בדמות עינו של הורוס נמצאו כבר על קברים קדומים המצרים, לצידם של מומיות."
- The Ankh is a cross with a loop at the top  
  _[it]_ ← "spesso rappresentavano la figura di un dio o l'Ankh, una croce ansata che rappresentava il simbolo della vita eterna, e l'Udjat, l'occhio di Horus simbolo di ri…"
- The Ankh symbolizes eternal life  
  _[it]_ ← "spesso rappresentavano la figura di un dio o l'Ankh, una croce ansata che rappresentava il simbolo della vita eterna, e l'Udjat, l'occhio di Horus simbolo di ri…"
- The Udjat symbolizes regeneration  
  _[it]_ ← "spesso rappresentavano la figura di un dio o l'Ankh, una croce ansata che rappresentava il simbolo della vita eterna, e l'Udjat, l'occhio di Horus simbolo di ri…"
- The scarab beetle figure found many supporters in the Western world  
  _[it]_ ← "Anche la figura dello scarabeo, rappresentante il dio Khepri, è diventata un comune amuleto, e anche nel mondo occidentale ha trovato molti "sostenitori"."
- The Shen was a common amulet  
  _[it]_ ← "Molto diffusi erano anche lo shen e il Nodo di Iside."
- The Knot of Isis was a common amulet  
  _[it]_ ← "Molto diffusi erano anche lo shen e il Nodo di Iside."
- 57 animal teeth were found in the priest's grave  
  _[lt]_ ← "Duonkalnio kapinyne prie Biržulio ežero žynio kape rasti 57, o 50–60 m. vyro kape – 83 žvėrių dantys."
- 83 animal teeth were found in the grave of a man aged 50 to 60 years old  
  _[lt]_ ← "Duonkalnio kapinyne prie Biržulio ežero žynio kape rasti 57, o 50–60 m. vyro kape – 83 žvėrių dantys."
- Amulets were found near the skull  
  _[lt]_ ← "Jų yra prie kaukolės, ant kaklo ir krūtinės."
- Amulets were found on the neck  
  _[lt]_ ← "Jų yra prie kaukolės, ant kaklo ir krūtinės."
- Amulets were found on the chest  
  _[lt]_ ← "Jų yra prie kaukolės, ant kaklo ir krūtinės."
- Miniature objects were found in graves  
  _[lt]_ ← "Dar kapuose aptinkama įvairių daiktų miniatiūrų, skirtų mirusiųjų palydoms į anapilį."
- These miniature objects were intended to accompany the deceased into the afterlife  
  _[lt]_ ← "Dar kapuose aptinkama įvairių daiktų miniatiūrų, skirtų mirusiųjų palydoms į anapilį."
- Amulets were also placed in the graves of the dead  
  _[pl]_ ← "Często drobny przedmiot, pochodzenia naturalnego lub wytworzony, służący do magicznej obrony przed złymi duchami, ludźmi lub nieszczęściami: noszone na ciele lu…"
- The amulet originates from ancient Egypt  
  _[pl]_ ← "talizman palladion amulety starożytnego Egiptu "oko Fatimy" (nazar) oraz "ręka Fatimy" (chamsa) krzyż św. Benedykta, Cudowny Medalik"
- Ancient Egyptians considered the amulet a guide on the path to the kingdom of death  
  _[ro]_ ← "Amuleta era considerată de vechii egipteni o apărătoare împotriva bolilor și a oricărui rău, precum și călăuză pe drumul spre împărăția morții."
- Many amulets were discovered in the remaining tombs of Ancient Egypt  
  _[ro]_ ← "În urma săpăturilor arheologice în mormintele rămase din Egiptul Antic au fost descoperite foarte multe amulete, cele mai multe fiind figuri zoomorfe (capete de…"
- The discovery was made through archaeological excavations  
  _[ro]_ ← "În urma săpăturilor arheologice în mormintele rămase din Egiptul Antic au fost descoperite foarte multe amulete, cele mai multe fiind figuri zoomorfe (capete de…"
- Most of the amulets were zoomorphic figures  
  _[ro]_ ← "În urma săpăturilor arheologice în mormintele rămase din Egiptul Antic au fost descoperite foarte multe amulete, cele mai multe fiind figuri zoomorfe (capete de…"
- The zoomorphic amulets included animal heads  
  _[ro]_ ← "În urma săpăturilor arheologice în mormintele rămase din Egiptul Antic au fost descoperite foarte multe amulete, cele mai multe fiind figuri zoomorfe (capete de…"
- Some of the amulets were geometric figures  
  _[ro]_ ← "În urma săpăturilor arheologice în mormintele rămase din Egiptul Antic au fost descoperite foarte multe amulete, cele mai multe fiind figuri zoomorfe (capete de…"
- Amulets were worn in the ears  
  _[ro]_ ← "Amuletele erau purtate la gât, atârnate de coliere, la urechi, pe cercei și la mâini, împodobind brățările."
- Amulets were worn as earrings  
  _[ro]_ ← "Amuletele erau purtate la gât, atârnate de coliere, la urechi, pe cercei și la mâini, împodobind brățările."
- Ancient Egypt used knots as an amulet  
  _[ru]_ ← "Древний Египет: изображения священного скарабея, узел, глаз Гора Ассирийцы: досочки с заклинаниями Китайцы: монеты с отверстиями, магические формулы («фу») в та…"
- These special characteristics included strength  
  _[sl]_ ← "Nekateri so ščitili uporabnika pred posebnimi nevarnostmi, drugi so bili obdarjeni s posebnimi značilnostmi, kot so trdnost in ognjevitost."
- These special characteristics included fire resistance  
  _[sl]_ ← "Nekateri so ščitili uporabnika pred posebnimi nevarnostmi, drugi so bili obdarjeni s posebnimi značilnostmi, kot so trdnost in ognjevitost."
- Amulets were often shaped like plants  
  _[sl]_ ← "Pogosto so bili v obliki živali, rastlin, sakralnih objektov ali iz hieroglifskih simbolov."
- Amulets were often shaped like sacred objects  
  _[sl]_ ← "Pogosto so bili v obliki živali, rastlin, sakralnih objektov ali iz hieroglifskih simbolov."
- Amulets were often used in conjunction with poultices  
  _[sl]_ ← "Papirusi kažejo, da so se amuleti uporabljali v medicini, pogosto v povezavi z obkladki (zdravilne obleke, pogosto so se uporabljali vroči) ali drugi pripravki …"
- Amulets were used in conjunction with the recitation of spells  
  _[sl]_ ← "Papirusi kažejo, da so se amuleti uporabljali v medicini, pogosto v povezavi z obkladki (zdravilne obleke, pogosto so se uporabljali vroči) ali drugi pripravki …"
- Papyri indicate that amulets were used in medicine  
  _[sl]_ ← "Papirusi kažejo, da so se amuleti uporabljali v medicini, pogosto v povezavi z obkladki (zdravilne obleke, pogosto so se uporabljali vroči) ali drugi pripravki …"
- These papyrus amulets were folded  
  _[sl]_ ← "Včasih so papirusi, na katerih so bili napisani uroki tudi lahko delovali kot amulet in so jih zložili in jih nosili s seboj."
- These papyrus amulets were carried by the user  
  _[sl]_ ← "Včasih so papirusi, na katerih so bili napisani uroki tudi lahko delovali kot amulet in so jih zložili in jih nosili s seboj."
- People most frequently wore a protective amulet in the form of the Eye of Horus  
  _[sl]_ ← "Najpogosteje so nosili zaščitni amulet v obliki Horovega očesa."
- They wore it during their lifetime  
  _[sl]_ ← "Nosili so ga za časa življenja in se je pogosto pojavil na obroču in kot element ogrlice."
- This occurred during the mummification process  
  _[sl]_ ← "Bil je tudi na telesu pokojnika v procesu mumifikacije, da bi zaščitili rez, skozi katerega so bili odstranjeni notranji organi."
- The purpose was to protect the incision  
  _[sl]_ ← "Bil je tudi na telesu pokojnika v procesu mumifikacije, da bi zaščitili rez, skozi katerega so bili odstranjeni notranji organi."
- The incision was made to remove internal organs  
  _[sl]_ ← "Bil je tudi na telesu pokojnika v procesu mumifikacije, da bi zaščitili rez, skozi katerega so bili odstranjeni notranji organi."
- More spells in the Book of the Dead were dedicated to speaking through special amulets  
  _[sl]_ ← "Več urokov v Knjigi mrtvih je bilo namenjeno govorjenju preko posebnih amuletov, ki so bili nato dani v posebna mesta na telesu pokojnika."
- These special amulets were placed in specific locations on the mummy's body  
  _[sl]_ ← "Več urokov v Knjigi mrtvih je bilo namenjeno govorjenju preko posebnih amuletov, ki so bili nato dani v posebna mesta na telesu pokojnika."
- The scarab was an important burial amulet  
  _[sl]_ ← "Skarabej je bil pomemben pogrebni amulet, povezan s ponovnim rojstvom, srce skarabeja kot amulet pa je preprečil srcu govoriti proti umrlemu."
- The scarab was associated with rebirth  
  _[sl]_ ← "Skarabej je bil pomemben pogrebni amulet, povezan s ponovnim rojstvom, srce skarabeja kot amulet pa je preprečil srcu govoriti proti umrlemu."
- The heart scarab amulet prevented the heart from speaking against the deceased  
  _[sl]_ ← "Skarabej je bil pomemben pogrebni amulet, povezan s ponovnim rojstvom, srce skarabeja kot amulet pa je preprečil srcu govoriti proti umrlemu."
- The Kushite kings ruled Egypt as the 25th dynasty  
  _[sl]_ ← "Ta vrsta amuleta na sliki na desni strani, je simbol boga Amon Raja in izvira z Kushite kraljev, ki so vladali Egiptu kot 25. dinastija."
- This type of amulet is a symbol of the god Amun-Ra  
  _[sl]_ ← "Ta vrsta amuleta na sliki na desni strani, je simbol boga Amon Raja in izvira z Kushite kraljev, ki so vladali Egiptu kot 25. dinastija."
- This type of amulet originated from Kushite kings  
  _[sl]_ ← "Ta vrsta amuleta na sliki na desni strani, je simbol boga Amon Raja in izvira z Kushite kraljev, ki so vladali Egiptu kot 25. dinastija."
- The crowns of their heads often featured two cobras  
  _[sl]_ ← "Njihova čela kot ovnova glava sta pogosto krasili dve kobri, namesto ene kobre kot na glavi egipčanskih kraljev."
- This was different from the single cobra found on the heads of Egyptian kings  
  _[sl]_ ← "Njihova čela kot ovnova glava sta pogosto krasili dve kobri, namesto ene kobre kot na glavi egipčanskih kraljev."

### Christianity
- Images of saints were a common form of Christian amulets  
  _[cs]_ ← "Do křesťanství amulety pronikly zejména v podobě křížku, nošeného na řetízku na krku, obrázků svatých a škapulířů s ostatky svatých a jinými věcmi, které byly p…"
- Scapulars containing relics of saints were a common form of Christian amulets  
  _[cs]_ ← "Do křesťanství amulety pronikly zejména v podobě křížku, nošeného na řetízku na krku, obrázků svatých a škapulířů s ostatky svatých a jinými věcmi, které byly p…"
- In medieval Europe, the Agnus Dei was considered a powerful amulet  
  _[cs]_ ← "Ve středověké Evropě byl za mocný amulet pokládán Agnus Dei (Beránek boží)."
- The medal was made by Cistercian monks  
  _[cs]_ ← "Jednalo se o medaili zhotovenou cisterciáckými mnichy v Jeruzalémě z vosku obětních svící Sixtinské kaple."
- The medal was made from the wax of votive candles  
  _[cs]_ ← "Jednalo se o medaili zhotovenou cisterciáckými mnichy v Jeruzalémě z vosku obětních svící Sixtinské kaple."
- The amulet was a medal  
  _[cs]_ ← "Jednalo se o medaili zhotovenou cisterciáckými mnichy v Jeruzalémě z vosku obětních svící Sixtinské kaple."
- The medal was made in Jerusalem  
  _[cs]_ ← "Jednalo se o medaili zhotovenou cisterciáckými mnichy v Jeruzalémě z vosku obětních svící Sixtinské kaple."
- The votive candles were from the Sistine Chapel  
  _[cs]_ ← "Jednalo se o medaili zhotovenou cisterciáckými mnichy v Jeruzalémě z vosku obětních svící Sixtinské kaple."
- The medallion bore the inscription 'Ecce Agnus dei, qui tollit peccata mundi'  
  _[cs]_ ← "Byl na ní zobrazen nápis Ecce Agnus dei, qui tollit peccata mundi ("Hle, Beránek boží, který snímá hříchy světa")."
- The amulet was intended to protect against evil spirits  
  _[cs]_ ← "Tento amulet měl chránit před zlými duchy, morem a jinými nemocemi atd."
- The amulet was intended to protect against the plague  
  _[cs]_ ← "Tento amulet měl chránit před zlými duchy, morem a jinými nemocemi atd."
- The amulet was intended to protect against other diseases  
  _[cs]_ ← "Tento amulet měl chránit před zlými duchy, morem a jinými nemocemi atd."
- The medal of the Virgin Mary has a similar function to an amulet  
  _[cs]_ ← "Podobnou funkci jako amulet má medailka Panny Marie (také zázračná medailka nebo medaile Neposkvrněného početí), o níž se traduje, že "ti, kdo ji budou s důvěro…"
- The medal of the Virgin Mary is also known as the miraculous medal  
  _[cs]_ ← "Podobnou funkci jako amulet má medailka Panny Marie (také zázračná medailka nebo medaile Neposkvrněného početí), o níž se traduje, že "ti, kdo ji budou s důvěro…"
- The medal of the Virgin Mary is also known as the medal of the Immaculate Conception  
  _[cs]_ ← "Podobnou funkci jako amulet má medailka Panny Marie (také zázračná medailka nebo medaile Neposkvrněného početí), o níž se traduje, že "ti, kdo ji budou s důvěro…"
- It is traditionally said that those who wear it with trust will receive many graces  
  _[cs]_ ← "Podobnou funkci jako amulet má medailka Panny Marie (také zázračná medailka nebo medaile Neposkvrněného početí), o níž se traduje, že "ti, kdo ji budou s důvěro…"
- The Catholic Church does not use the term 'cross worn around the neck'  
  _[cs]_ ← "Křížek nošený na krku je asi celosvětově nejrozšířenější amulet, i když katolická církev tento název nepoužívá."
- The cross worn around the neck is one of the most widespread amulets globally  
  _[cs]_ ← "Křížek nošený na krku je asi celosvětově nejrozšířenější amulet, i když katolická církev tento název nepoužívá."
- The Catholic Church associates the cross necklace with paganism  
  _[cs]_ ← "Ta naopak amulet zakazuje a spojuje ho s pohanstvím a čarodějnictvím."

- A scapular is a votive offering

- In medieval Europe, the Christian Church opposed superstition  
  _[de]_ ← "In Europa wandte sich die christliche Kirche im Mittelalter ebenfalls gegen den Aberglauben, zu dem auch Amulette gerechnet wurden."
- Amulets were considered a form of superstition  
  _[de]_ ← "In Europa wandte sich die christliche Kirche im Mittelalter ebenfalls gegen den Aberglauben, zu dem auch Amulette gerechnet wurden."
- Popular belief did not stop people from holding onto amulets with a Christian connection  
  _[de]_ ← "Das hinderte den Volksglauben allerdings nicht daran, an Amuletten mit christlichem Bezug festzuhalten."
- High-ranking churchmen possess lucky charms  
  _[de]_ ← "Auch hohe Kirchenmänner besitzen Glücksbringer."
- The treasure inventory of the Holy See from 1295 recorded 15 adder-tongue ferns  
  _[de]_ ← "So sind etwa im Schatzinventar des Heiligen Stuhls von 1295 15 Natternzungenbäume verzeichnet."
- Anselm Franz von Würzburg was a lifelong opponent of superstition and witchcraft  
  _[de]_ ← "Als am 9. Februar 1749 der Fürstbischof Anselm Franz von Würzburg, zeitlebens ein Streiter gegen Aberglauben und Hexenwahn, nach einem Schlaganfall starb, fand …"
- An amulet made of brass sheet metal was found on his chest  
  _[de]_ ← "Als am 9. Februar 1749 der Fürstbischof Anselm Franz von Würzburg, zeitlebens ein Streiter gegen Aberglauben und Hexenwahn, nach einem Schlaganfall starb, fand …"

  _[de]_ ← "Als am 9. Februar 1749 der Fürstbischof Anselm Franz von Würzburg, zeitlebens ein Streiter gegen Aberglauben und Hexenwahn, nach einem Schlaganfall starb, fand …"
- The amulet had some magic formulas engraved on it  
  _[de]_ ← "Als am 9. Februar 1749 der Fürstbischof Anselm Franz von Würzburg, zeitlebens ein Streiter gegen Aberglauben und Hexenwahn, nach einem Schlaganfall starb, fand …"
- Pest talers were amulet medals  
  _[de]_ ← "Pesttaler als talerförmige Amulettmedaillen"
- Christian authorities have always maintained a certain distance from amulets  
  _[es]_ ← "Las autoridades cristianas han mantenido siempre cierta distancia con los amuletos y otros talismanes."
- Some Christians currently place rosaries in their vehicle rearview mirrors  
  _[es]_ ← "Al igual que actualmente algunos cristianos colocan en los retrovisores de sus vehículos rosarios o imágenes de San Cristóbal como una manera de propiciar la pr…"
